The absence of psychotic symptoms (such as hearing voices or seeing things that aren't there) reduces the likelihood of Schizoaffective Disorder (Bipolar Type). Subtypes: Occupational Hyperactivity, Social Hyperactivity, Hypersexuality, Psychomotor Agitation\n[S041] Painful_Consequence_Behavior: Excessive involvement in activities that have a high potential for painful consequences.
